\"\"\"\n\n    patterns = [\n        # <hp:linesegarray>...</hp:linesegarray> (any prefix)\n        re.compile(\n            r\"<(?P<tag>[A-Za-z0-9_:\\-.]*:?linesegarray)\\b[^>]*>.*?</(?P=tag)>\",\n
